     1→# SHOPMESH Build Worklog
     2→
     3→Project: SHOPMESH v1 — multi-tenant commerce platform (storefronts + page builder + marketplace + commission engine + double-entry ledger + payouts + admin command centre), built from the PART0..PART6 blueprint in /home/z/my-project/upload/.
     4→
     5→Sandbox adaptations (decided, do not change):
     6→- Single Next.js 16 app (App Router) on port 3000. Single user-visible route `/`; all surfaces (Shopper / Seller / Admin) are client-side surfaces switched in src/components/app-root.tsx via zustand (src/lib/app-store.ts).
     7→- SQLite + Prisma (db/custom.db) instead of PostgreSQL. Money = Int minor units (cents). JSONB → String JSON columns (src/lib/json.ts). Enums → String + app-level validation.
     8→- No Redis/Meili/BullMQ: workers simulated inline (webhook deliveries recorded on demand, notifications logged to notification_logs, wallet cache refreshed on write).
     9→- Mock payment gateway: checkout creates PaymentIntent with redirectUrl /pay/{providerRef}; a mock hosted payment page (part of shopper surface) POSTs a signed webhook to /api/webhooks/payments/{provider}; API verifies HMAC signature, logs to payment_webhook_logs, marks order paid, posts ledger T1.
    10→- Auth: cookie session (sm_session) stored in Session table. /api/auth/* already implemented. Demo credentials seeded.
    11→- Placeholder images: GET /api/img/{seed}?w=&h=&label= returns deterministic SVG. Use it for ALL demo imagery (never external URLs).
    12→
    13→## Task 1 — Foundation (DONE, by orchestrator)
    14→
    15→Files created (DO NOT MODIFY except where noted):
    16→- prisma/schema.prisma — full data model (~50 models). db pushed.
    17→- prisma/seed.ts — rich demo data (5 sellers/stores, 27 products, 14 orders, 18 balanced ledger txns, payout batch, coupons, banners, tickets, audit logs). Run: `bun prisma/seed.ts` (idempotent, wipes first).
    18→- src/lib/db.ts (prisma client, `import { db } from "@/lib/db"`)
    19→- src/lib/money.ts — formatMinor(minor, currency="MYR"), toMinor(input), commissionOf(base, percent) [round-half-up], allocateProportionally(amount, weights[]), divHalfUp
    20→- src/lib/json.ts — parseJson<T>(raw, fallback), toJson(value)
    21→- src/lib/commission.ts — M09 engine: getCommissionSettings(), resolveRate({sellerId, categoryId, productId, now}) → {percent, source: fee_holiday|product|category|seller|global, holdDays, fixedFeeMinor}, computeOrderCommissions({sellerId, items[], orderDiscountMinor, shippingMinorTotal, now}) → {items: [{discountMinor, shippingMinor, baseMinor, percent, percentPartMinor, fixedFeeMinor, commissionMinor, sellerNetMinor, breakdown}], orderCommissionMinor, orderSellerNetMinor, holdDays, holdReleaseRule}, simulateCommission(...)
    22→- src/lib/ledger.ts — M10 engine: ACCOUNTS.*, ensureWalletAccounts(walletId), postTransaction({txnType, refType, refId, description, lines[]}) [throws if unbalanced], journal recipes: postOrderPaid, postGatewaySettlement, postPayoutInitiated, postPayoutConfirmed, postPayoutFailed, postRefund, postAdminAdjustment; walletTruth(walletId) [pending=credits availableAt>now, available=matured credits−debits], refreshWalletCache(walletId), runLedgerInvariants(), buildPayoutStatement(...)
    23→- src/lib/password.ts — hashPassword, verifyPassword, randomToken, hmacSha256Hex(secret, payload)
    24→- src/lib/auth.ts — createSession(userType, userId), destroySession(), getSession(), requireAdmin() → AdminUserDTO, requireAdminRole(roles), requireCap(cap) [RBAC matrix], requireSeller() → SellerUserDTO {id, email, fullName, displayName, status, storeId, storeSlug, storeName}, requireActiveSellerStore() → {me, store}
    25→- src/lib/api.ts — ApiError(status, code, msg), ok(data), okPaged(rows, {page,size,total}), fail(status, code, msg, details?), handle(fn) [route wrapper — ALWAYS wrap handlers], getPagination(url) → {page,size,skip,take}, auditAdmin({adminId, adminEmail, action, entityType, entityId, before, after}), logActivity(...), notify({event, channel, recipient, subject}), dispatchStoreWebhooks({storeId, event, payload}), transitionOrder({orderId, toStatus, actor, note, timestamps}) [writes history], slugify(s), nextOrderNumber() → "SM-100xxx"
    26→- src/lib/sections.ts — SECTION_REGISTRY (15 section types with defaultProps + builder fields), createSection(type), parsePageDoc(json) → {sections, version}, parseTheme(json) → ThemeTokens, DEFAULT_THEME, defaultHomeSections(storeName, tagline), defaultPageSections(pageType, storeName). Page doc JSON shape: {sections:[{id, type, props}], version}
    27→- src/lib/app-store.ts — zustand: useApp() {surface: 'shopper'|'seller'|'admin', setSurface, activeStoreSlug, setActiveStoreSlug, me, refreshMe(), logout()}
    28→- src/components/app-root.tsx — surface switcher + lazy shells (imports the 3 shells; do not rename files)
    29→- src/app/page.tsx — renders <AppRoot/>
    30→- src/app/api/img/[seed]/route.ts, src/app/api/auth/{me,login,logout,register}/route.ts, src/app/api/health/route.ts

    38→- Dev server already runs on port 3000 in background (log: /home/z/my-project/dev.log — read the TAIL only). Do not run `bun run build`. Use `bun run lint` from /home/z/my-project to check your files.

    46→## API CONTRACT (v1) — implemented by Task 2-a (admin+auth-adjacent) and Task 2-b (store/market/checkout)
    47→
    48→Envelope: {data} | {data, meta:{page,size,total}} | {error:{code,message,details}}.
    49→Auth: cookie session. Admin endpoints require admin session (401 otherwise). Seller endpoints require seller session. Public endpoints listed as public.
    50→
    51→### Task 2-a scope — /api/admin/** (all require admin session unless noted)
    52→- GET  /api/admin/dashboard → KPI object {gmvTodayMinor, ordersToday, commissionTodayMinor, activeStores, pendingKyc, productsToModerate, refundsQueue, disputesOpen, payoutsDueMinor, payoutsDueSellers, gmvSeries30d: [{date, gmvMinor, orders}], topStores: [{storeName, gmv30dMinor, orders30d}], recentSellers: [{id, displayName, storeName, status, createdAt}]}
    53→- GET  /api/admin/sellers?status=&search=&riskTier=&page=&size= → Seller[] w/ store, wallet, gmv30dMinor
    54→- GET  /api/admin/sellers/[id] → {seller, store, kycDocuments, bankAccounts, wallet, products(5 recent), orders(5 recent), notes, gmv30dMinor}
    55→- PATCH /api/admin/sellers/[id] {riskTier?, commissionOverridePercent?, holdDaysOverride?, marketplaceOptIn?} (audit)
    56→- POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/approve  (sets seller active, store live, kyc approved, notify kyc.approved) — RBAC sellers.approve
    57→- POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/reject {reason} → status rejected
    58→- POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/suspend {reason?} / POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/activate  (store status live/draft) — RBAC sellers.suspend
    59→- POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/terminate {reason?}
    60→- POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/kyc/[docId] {decision: approved|rejected, reason?} (audit; auto-approve seller when all docs approved + bank verified + ≥1 product)
    61→- GET/POST /api/admin/sellers/[id]/notes {body}
    62→- GET  /api/admin/kyc-queue → sellers pending_review w/ docs + bank + products (cards)
    63→- GET  /api/admin/products?status=pending_review|all&search=&storeId=&page= → products w/ images, store name, flags
    64→- POST /api/admin/products/[id]/approve | /reject {reason?} | /ban {reason?} (audit; RBAC products.moderate)
    65→- GET/POST /api/admin/categories {name, parentId?} ; PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/categories/[id] {name?, commissionPercent?} (delete nulls product.categoryId)
    66→- GET  /api/admin/orders?status=&storeId=&search=&page= ; GET /api/admin/orders/[id] → {order, items, history, paymentIntents, refunds, dispute}
    67→- GET  /api/admin/refunds?status= ; POST /api/admin/refunds/[id]/approve ; POST /api/admin/refunds/[id]/process → posts ledger T5 postRefund (commission reversal = round(refund * item.commissionPercent/100) using the order's largest refunded item), sets item refundedMinor, order status refunded/partially_refunded, restocks variants if restock, webhook dispatch order.refunded
    68→- POST /api/admin/refunds/[id]/reject {reason?}
    69→- GET  /api/admin/disputes ; PATCH /api/admin/disputes/[id] {status, resolution?} (resolve clears order.disputedFlag)
    70→- GET/PUT /api/admin/commission/settings (PUT audit; validate percent 0..100)
    71→- GET/POST /api/admin/commission/overrides ; PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/commission/overrides/[id]
    72→- GET/POST /api/admin/commission/holidays ; PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/commission/holidays/[id]
    73→- POST /api/admin/commission/simulate → simulateCommission(body) — PUBLIC? no, admin-only
    74→- GET  /api/admin/wallets → wallets w/ seller + store names (recompute via refreshWalletCache per wallet)
    75→- POST /api/admin/wallets/[id]/adjust {amountMinor, direction: credit|debit, reason} → postAdminAdjustment T6 + audit — RBAC payouts.manage
    76→- GET  /api/admin/ledger/transactions?type=&cursor=&walletId= → txns w/ lines (last 100 ok, cursor by id desc)
    77→- GET  /api/admin/ledger/accounts → accounts with balance (sum debit vs credit)
    78→- GET  /api/admin/payouts?status=&batchId= ; POST /api/admin/payouts/[id]/mark-paid {referenceNo} → T4 + status paid + notify payout.paid ; POST /api/admin/payouts/[id]/fail {reason} → T4f reversal + status failed
    79→- GET  /api/admin/payout-batches ; POST /api/admin/payout-batches {dryRun?: boolean} → dryRun: preview wallets ≥ min (setting payout.min_minor) w/o writing; else create batch + payouts(processing) + T3 per wallet + statementJson; payouts status processing, method = setting payout.mode — RBAC payouts.manage
    80→- GET  /api/admin/banks?verified=false ; POST /api/admin/banks/[id]/verify | /reject
    81→- GET/POST /api/admin/coupons ; PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/coupons/[id]
    82→- GET/POST /api/admin/banners ; PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/banners/[id]
    83→- GET/POST /api/admin/featured {productId, slot?} ; DELETE /api/admin/featured/[id]
    84→- GET/POST /api/admin/announcements ; PATCH /api/admin/announcements/[id]
    85→- GET  /api/admin/audit-logs?adminEmail=&action=&entityType=&page= ; GET /api/admin/activity-logs?actorType=&page=
    86→- GET/PUT /api/admin/settings (key/valueJson list; PUT upserts, audit) — RBAC settings.manage
    87→- GET/PATCH /api/admin/feature-flags {key, isEnabled?, rolloutPercent?}
    88→- GET  /api/admin/system/health → counts, runLedgerInvariants(), latest activity, db ok
    89→- GET  /api/admin/notifications (notification logs, latest 100)
    90→- GET  /api/admin/webhook-deliveries ; POST /api/admin/webhook-deliveries/[id]/replay (mark pending→success sim)
    91→- GET  /api/admin/tickets?status= ; GET /api/admin/tickets/[id] (w/ messages) ; POST /api/admin/tickets/[id]/reply {body} ; PATCH /api/admin/tickets/[id] {status, priority?}
    92→- GET  /api/admin/reports/[type]?days=30 for type in sales|sellers|commission|payouts → aggregated series+rows
    93→- GET  /api/admin/stores?search= → stores w/ seller + product counts
    94→- GET  /api/admin/export → CSV export (simple: return text/csv of requested entity, e.g. ?entity=orders) — keep simple
    95→
    96→### Task 2-b scope — /api/store/**, /api/market/**, /api/sf/**, /api/cart, /api/checkout/**, /api/webhooks/**, /api/orders/lookup
    97→Seller (require seller session; store scoped):
    98→- GET  /api/store/me → {seller, store, wallet, onboarding: {step, checklist: {profile, storeIdentity, kyc, bank, products}}, stats}
    99→- POST /api/store/onboarding {action: "submit"} → seller.status=pending_review, submittedAt (notify admin email log)
   100→- PATCH /api/store/settings {name?, tagline?, description?, supportEmail?, supportPhone?, whatsapp?, marketplaceOptIn?, logoUrl?, bannerUrl?, social?} (also updates cms header if needed)
   101→- GET/POST /api/store/products (create w/ images[], variants[]; first 5 products of seller → pending_review per setting moderation.first_n, else active unless banned keywords [list: "replica","fake","gun","drug"] → flags) ; GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/store/products/[id] (delete = archive)
   102→- POST /api/store/products/[id]/variants {name, optionName?, optionValue?, priceMinor, stock?, sku?} ; PATCH/DELETE /api/store/variants/[id] ; POST /api/store/inventory/adjust {variantId, delta, reason} → InventoryLog + stock.low notify when below threshold
   103→- GET  /api/store/orders?status=&search= ; GET /api/store/orders/[id]
   104→- POST /api/store/orders/[id]/fulfill → processing ; POST /api/store/orders/[id]/ship {provider, trackingNumber} → shipped + dispatchStoreWebhooks order.shipped + notify customer email
   105→- POST /api/store/orders/[id]/refund-request {amountMinor, reason?, restock?} → Refund requested (notify admin)
   106→- GET  /api/store/wallet → {wallet(truth-refreshed), recentLines: ledger lines for wallet account (desc 20), payouts}
   107→- GET  /api/store/payouts ; GET/POST /api/store/bank-accounts {holderName, bankName, accountNumber}
   108→- GET  /api/store/pages ; PUT /api/store/pages/[id]/draft {sections} (bump draftVersion, hasUnpublished=true) ; POST /api/store/pages/[id]/publish (publishedJson=draftJson, publishedVersion++, hasUnpublished=false) ; POST /api/store/pages/[id]/revert (draft=published)
   109→- GET/PUT /api/store/theme {theme}
   110→- GET/POST /api/store/coupons (store scope) ; PATCH/DELETE /api/store/coupons/[id]
   111→- GET  /api/store/collections ; POST /api/store/collections {name} ; POST /api/store/collections/[id]/products {productIds}
   112→- GET/PUT /api/store/integrations {integrations: [{provider, config, isEnabled}]} (upsert per provider)
   113→- GET/POST /api/store/webhooks ; PATCH/DELETE /api/store/webhooks/[id] ; POST /api/store/webhooks/[id]/test → creates a demo delivery
   114→- GET  /api/store/domains ; POST /api/store/domains {domain, mode} (status verifying) ; POST /api/store/domains/[id]/verify → simulate: status verified→active, ssl issued (notify domain.verified) ; DELETE /api/store/domains/[id]
   115→- GET  /api/store/dashboard → today/7d/30d cards {orders, revenueMinor, commissionMinor, netMinor}, lowStock: variants stock<=threshold, recentOrders 5, pendingActions
   116→
   117→Public market/storefront:
   118→- GET  /api/market/home → {banners, categories(tree, 2 levels), featured: ProductCard[], latest: ProductCard[], topStores: StoreCard[]}
   119→- GET  /api/market/products?search=&category=&sort=newest|price_asc|price_desc|popular&page= → ProductCard[] paged (filter: product.status=active, store.status=live, store marketplaceOptIn) ProductCard = {id, slug, title, priceMinor, compareAtPriceMinor, imageUrl, storeName, storeSlug, ratingAvg, ratingCount, salesCount, categoryName}
   120→- GET  /api/market/stores → StoreCard[] {id, name, slug, tagline, logoUrl, bannerUrl, productCount}
   121→- GET  /api/sf/[slug] → store home: {store: {name, slug, tagline, logoUrl, theme(parsed), whatsapp, supportEmail}, page: {title, sections(parsed), publishedVersion}, products for grid sections resolved? NO — client fetches products per section via /api/sf/[slug]/products?source=&limit=&collection=
   122→- GET  /api/sf/[slug]/products?search=&collection=&source=latest|featured&limit= → ProductCard[]
   123→- GET  /api/sf/[slug]/products/[pSlug] → PDP: {product: {id, title, slug, description, images[], variants[], priceMinor, compareAtPriceMinor, ratingAvg, ratingCount}, reviews[], related[6], store:{name, slug, tagline, whatsapp}}
   124→- POST /api/sf/[slug]/products/[pSlug]/reviews {customerName, rating, title?, body?} → recompute product rating
   125→- GET  /api/sf/[slug]/pages/[pageSlug] → {store, page, sections} for about/contact/policy
   126→- POST /api/sf/[slug]/newsletter {email} → notification log + activity log
   127→
   128→Cart & checkout (guest; cart token cookie sm_cart; one cart per store):
   129→- GET  /api/cart → current cart w/ items (variant+product+store joined) — creates empty if missing? return null-safe {cart: {items:[]}} when empty
   130→- POST /api/cart/items {variantId, qty} → adds (sets cart storeId from variant), PATCH /api/cart/items/[id] {qty}, DELETE /api/cart/items/[id]
   131→- POST /api/checkout/quote {storeSlug, items:[{variantId, qty}], couponCode?, shippingRateId?} → server recompute: {subtotalMinor, discountMinor, shippingMinor, totalMinor, coupon: {code, valid, reason?}, items:[{title, variantLabel, qty, unitPriceMinor, lineTotalMinor}], commissionPreviewMinor?} — coupon validation: window/active/minSpend/usage limits (platform+store coupons)
   132→- POST /api/checkout/create (Idempotency-Key header; if Order with same idemKey exists return it) {storeSlug, items, customer:{name,email,phone}, shippingAddress, couponCode?, provider, customerNote?} → validates stock, recomputes via computeOrderCommissions (snapshot), decrements stock, creates Order(payment_pending)+items+intent(providerRef ref_sm{rand}, redirectUrl /pay/{providerRef}, TTL 30min), history row, notify admin email "New order", returns {orderNumber, orderPaymentRef}
   133→- GET  /api/checkout/payments/[ref] → {status: requires_payment|succeeded|failed, orderNumber, amountMinor, storeName}
   134→- POST /api/webhooks/payments/[provider] — body {providerRef, eventType: payment.succeeded|payment.failed, amountMinor, ts, sig}. Verify sig = hmacSha256Hex("sm_webhook_secret_demo", `${providerRef}.${eventType}.${amountMinor}.${ts}`) header X-SM-Signature (ALSO accept sig in body.sig). Log PaymentWebhookLog (raw). Idempotent by providerRef (if intent already succeeded → return ok). On payment.succeeded: intent succeeded, transitionOrder paid (paidAt), T1 postOrderPaid (wallet from store.seller, holdUntil = paidAt + holdDays from commission settings), coupon redemption record + timesUsed++, product salesCount++ (done at create? NO — do here), dispatchStoreWebhooks order.paid, notify seller email+inapp + customer email. On failed: intent failed.
   135→- GET  /api/orders/lookup?email=&orderNumber= → order status for shoppers (guest order tracking)
   136→
   137→Payment providers list (for checkout UI): [{id: "fpx_toyyibpay", label: "FPX Online Banking"}, {id: "tng", label: "Touch 'n Go eWallet"}, {id: "grabpay", label: "GrabPay"}, {id: "card_stripe", label: "Card (Stripe MY)"}]. Webhook secret for the mock pay page: "sm_webhook_secret_demo".
   138→

   141→### CONTRACT ADDENDUM (sandbox mock payment)
   142→- POST /api/checkout/payments/[ref]/simulate {result: "succeeded"|"failed"} (public; part of Task 2-b)
   143→  Server-side only: builds the webhook payload, signs it with hmacSha256Hex("sm_webhook_secret_demo", `${ref}.${eventType}.${amountMinor}.${ts}`), and internally invokes the same verification+processing logic as /api/webhooks/payments/[provider] (HMAC secret NEVER exposed to client). The mock hosted payment page in the shopper surface calls this endpoint. Returns {status, orderNumber}.

   309→
   310→Stage Summary:
   311→- VERDICT: URL-only (unauthorised) catalogue import is NOT possible on either platform — all catalogue APIs require seller authorization; both platforms' policies forbid unauthorised content access (Shopee DPP "Shopee Content" ownership; TikTok Developer ToS §3.5). Design must be authorization-first; pasted URLs serve only as shop identification.
   312→- Spec's Shopee link v2.ams.get_open_campaign_added_product = Affiliate Marketing Solution open-campaign API (commission rates only, AMS T&C gated) — NOT a catalogue import API. Correct Shopee path: product.get_item_list (offset/page_size≤100) → get_item_base_info (≤50 ids, name/desc/images/prices) → get_model_list (variants w/ tier_index, price_info, stock_info_v2) → shop.get_shop_info (region check).
   313→- TikTok correct path: GET /authorization/202309/shops (shop_cipher) → POST /product/202309/products/search (page_size≤100 + opaque page_token; scope seller.product.basic) → GET /product/202309/products/{id} (full schema: main_images, HTML description, skus[] w/ sales_attributes, inventory, list_price).
   314→- Signing verified: Shopee = HMAC-SHA256(partner_key, partner_id+path+timestamp[+access_token+shop_id]) hex; TikTok = hex HMAC-SHA256(app_secret, secret + sorted{key}{value} + path + body + secret), token in x-tts-access-token header, timestamp window [now−5m, now+30s], shop_cipher required on product endpoints.
   315→- Token lifecycle verified: Shopee code(10min)→access 4h + refresh 30d single-use (rotate), auth validity ≤365d; TikTok auth_code 30min → access 7d, grant_type literal "authorized_code", refresh = granted duration, expiry webhook T-30d, host auth.tiktok-shops.com/api/v2/token/{get,refresh}.
   316→- MY market support confirmed on both (Shopee seller eligibility table incl. MY; TikTok region MY/ms-MY/MYR/COD).
   317→- Rate limits: TikTok dynamic QPS documented (3–10 rps baseline read per App×Shop; 429/36009002); Shopee NOT published on accessible pages → UNRESOLVED, conservative throttle recommended.
   318→- Env vars for deployer: Shopee SHOPEE_PARTNER_ID/SHOPEE_PARTNER_KEY/SHOPEE_HOST/SHOPEE_REDIRECT_URI; TikTok TIKTOK_APP_KEY/TIKTOK_APP_SECRET/TIKTOK_REDIRECT_URI (+auth/token/api hosts); per-shop tokens belong in DB (MarketplaceConnection), not env.
   319→- Blockers: no credentials in sandbox (cannot live-test); Shopee rate-limit table not publicly accessible; TikTok Partner Center pages are SPA-only for browsers (content API used instead).

   331→- Deliverable written: docs/research-tiktok.md (9 sections per task spec, source URL per claim).
   332→
   333→Stage Summary:
   334→- Base host for ALL OpenAPI calls (US+ROW): https://open-api.tiktokglobalshop.com; token host https://auth.tiktok-shops.com/api/v2/token/{get,refresh}; seller auth link https://services.tiktokshop.com/open/authorize?service_id={service_id} (US adds .us).
   335→- grant_type is literally "authorized_code" (NOT authorization_code); auth_code expires 30 min, single use.
   336→- Access token default validity 7 days (x-tts-access-token header, 202309+); refresh_token valid = authorization duration; 30-day expiry warning webhook (UPCOMING_AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRATION) then daily; revoke → SELLER_DEAUTHORIZATION.
   337→- Signing = HMAC-SHA256(secret + path + sorted{key}{value} + raw_body(except multipart) + secret), excluding sign & access_token; timestamp 10-digit unix, window [now-5min, now+30s].
   338→- Common params: app_key, sign, timestamp (query) + x-tts-access-token (header) + shop_cipher (product APIs; from Get Authorized Shops = /authorization/202309/shops → shops[]{id,region,seller_type,cipher}).
   339→- Products read scope = seller.product.basic ("Product Basic"); even Public scopes still require seller authorization → URL-only/unauthorised import NOT possible (verdict documented with 4 evidence points).
   340→- Search Products: POST /product/202309/products/search; page_size REQUIRED 1-100; page_token ← next_page_token; total_count; NO has_more; hard cap 10,000 results (err 12052180) → need incremental time-window sync (create_time_ge/le, update_time_ge/le).
   341→- Get Product: GET /product/202309/products/{product_id}; full schema captured: title, description (HTML), brand{id,name}, category_chains[], main_images[]{urls,thumb_urls,uri,w,h}, video{url,cover_url}, package_dimensions/weight, skus[]{id, seller_sku, price{currency,sale_price,tax_exclusive_price}, inventory[]{warehouse_id,quantity(1-99999)}, sales_attributes[]{name,value_name,sku_img(variant image)}, status_info}, audit block.
   342→- Status enum: DRAFT/PENDING/FAILED/ACTIVATE/SELLER_DEACTIVATED/PLATFORM_DEACTIVATED/FREEZE/DELETED/SCHEDULED.
   343→- Rate limits: dynamic QPS (no fixed numbers); isolation unit App ID × Authorized Shop; signals HTTP 429 + business code 36009002; planning baselines per app-shop: reads 3-10 (light 5-20) rps, writes 1-3, heavy 0.2-1; backoff+jitter, honour Retry-After.
   344→- Markets: 16 regions incl. MY (locale ms-MY, currency MYR, COD flag) on the single global API host.
   345→- Webhooks: PRODUCT_CREATION / PRODUCT_INFORMATION_CHANGE / PRODUCT_STATUS_CHANGE / PRODUCT_CATEGORY_CHANGE / PRODUCT_AUDIT_STATUS_CHANGE; signature = HMAC-SHA256(app_key+raw_body, app_secret) in Authorization header (no Bearer); docs mandate polling backup.
   346→- App registration: Partner Center → App & Service; credentials App Key / App Secret / Service ID; Custom apps skip review UNLESS Connector category or ≥25 seller authorizations (our connector case → review expected); env vars proposed: TIKTOK_SHOP_APP_KEY/APP_SECRET/REDIRECT_URI/WEBHOOK_URL.
   347→- Error codes verified: 105005 scope denied, 105002 token expired, 106001 bad sign, 36009004 family (timestamp/sign/key/shop_cipher), 106013 missing shop_cipher, 36004004 bad auth_code, 36009033 IP allowlist.

   365→- Catalogue chain [VERIFIED]: product.get_item_list (offset/page_size≤100/item_status required; has_next_page+next_offset) → product.get_item_base_info (≤50 ids; item_name, description, price_info, image.image_url_list, stock_info_v2, condition, item_status, has_model, category_id) → product.get_model_list (tier_variation[] options+option images; model[] model_id/price_info/stock_info_v2/model_sku/model_status/tier_index) → shop.get_shop_info (shop_name/region/status/auth/expire) + shop.get_profile (shop_logo).
   366→- URL-only import verdict: NO — every catalogue API is a Shop API requiring seller authorisation (explicit guide quote); no URL/slug-based public endpoint exists (public.* = token mgmt + get_shops_by_partner only); ISV rules forbid services that extract listings off-platform → connector must be authorisation-code-flow based.
   367→- Spec's campaign endpoint assessed: v2.ams.get_open_campaign_added_product retrieves only products the seller enrolled in the affiliate Open Campaign (commission_rate/campaign_status/periods), is restricted to "Affiliate Marketing Solution Management" app type and gated by AMS T&C acceptance — NOT a catalogue importer; recommend the get_item_list chain instead.
   368→- MY market: supported (eligibility Mall OR Registered Business OR ≥1 order in L12M; category languages en/ms-my/zh-hans; 2-decimal pricing; region field "MY").
   369→- Rate limits: only HTTP 429 + "adjust QPS" guidance published — no numeric table; plan self-throttling + backoff. Hosts: partner.shopeemobile.com (prod), openplatform.sandbox.test-stable.shopee.sg (sandbox).

   578→Stage Summary:
   579→- KEY CONCLUSION for Option A: marketplace collect-then-disburse is permissible WITHOUT an e-money licence if (a) buyer funds are collected via a BNM-registered merchant acquirer/gateway, (b) held only in a designated trust/client account segregated from working capital, and (c) disbursed to Malaysian sellers as ordinary bank transfers. EMI approval under FSA is triggered only if SHOPMESH issues its own open-loop stored value (MeshPay wallet); coins/loyalty points are typically limited-purpose and exempt (verify design). Cross-border payouts to foreign sellers require MSBA Class B remittance licence OR a partner licensed remitter/PSP. Consumer Credit Act 2025 now regulates BNPL (SKP, licensing from 1 Jun 2026) - do not launch SPayLater-like features before registration/partnership. CPETTR 2024 imposes direct duties on the marketplace operator (seller info verification + Bahasa Malaysia display) in force since 25 Dec 2024. Full 9-topic report with URLs delivered to orchestrator; nothing in the codebase was modified.

  1041→Work Log:
  1042→- ROOT CAUSE of the delay: setQty/remove awaited the PATCH/DELETE, then notifyCartUpdated() made EVERY mounted useCart() instance refetch /api/cart (2+ RTTs per click, busy-lock dimming the row meanwhile). Same disease Task 28 cured for checkboxes.
  1043→- INSTANT QTY/REMOVE: rewritten as optimistic shared-store mutations (mutateCartItems) — row, line price, subtotal, free-ship bar, header count and badge repaint in the click frame with ZERO network in the render path. Rapid +/- taps are coalesced into ONE PATCH per item (350ms trailing debounce — 5 taps = 1 request). Server clamps are adopted only if the buyer hasn't tapped since; failures roll back to the pre-burst qty (guarded so a revert can never clobber a newer edit). Remove animates out instantly, DELETE rides behind, guarded re-insert + toast on failure. busyId lock removed from the row entirely.
  1044→- MOQ (new capability): ProductVariant.moq Int @default(1) pushed to Supabase; seller Product editor gained a "Min. order" column per variant (products.tsx Variant type + payload + grid col; validateVariants accepts/normalizes moq; min 1). PDP payload carries moq → stepper min = moq, "Min. order N" chip, add posts max(qty, moq). Cart add below MOQ → adjusted UP with notice; MOQ beyond stock → honest 409. Cart PATCH below MOQ → 409 MOQ_NOT_MET (qty 0 still removes). Cart drawer shows amber "Seller minimum N — increase quantity" chip when a seller raises the minimum after the item was added; minus is floor-limited at moq (trash remains the removal path).
  1045→- STOCK PROBE (the "special method"): NEW GET /api/cart/stock?ids=… — one PK-indexed query, ~few hundred bytes, no joins, no writes; fires ONCE per drawer open (seq-guarded), merges stock/moq/purchasable into the shared store and clamps qty LOCALLY (qty>stock → adjust + toast "Stock changed while you were away"; stock 0 → "Out of stock" chip). Layer 1 of 3: probe → client clamp → authoritative atomic decrement.
  1046→- OVERSELL-PROOF CHECKOUT (influencer-drop guard): checkout/create's plain `update decrement` replaced with a CONDITIONAL decrement — `updateMany({ where: { id, isActive, stock: { gte: qty } }, data: { decrement } })`; count=0 → 409 OUT_OF_STOCK → the ENTIRE transaction (orders, items, vouchers, ledger) rolls back. Under READ COMMITTED Postgres evaluates the row-locked condition atomically, so exactly ONE racing checkout wins — stock can never go negative, no reservation tables, no locks to manage, one UPDATE per line (cheapest correctness at any concurrency). Friendly pre-check (incl. MOQ) kept for fast-fail messages.
  1047→- QUOTE stays renderable: PricedLine now carries moq; quote responses expose it; the checkout client shows an amber "seller minimum" warning box (mirroring the oversell box), blocks Pay, offers "Update cart". Hard gate remains in create.
  1048→- LAYOUT per spec: cart row = [checkbox][img][title → price directly under it → variant/chips][right column: trash on top, minimalist stepper under it]. "RM X each" removed (price shown is the live line total, recomputed in the same optimistic mutation). NEW QtyStepper `xs` size: h-6 w-6 buttons, w-6 field, 11px digits, h-3 icons, rounded-md. Seller Cart Experience preview mirrored (price under name, trash+mini stepper right column).
  1049→- Add-to-cart paths (PDP + drawer upsell) now adopt the server cart response via applyServerCart() — same-frame badge/drawer sync, zero refetch (notifyCartUpdated removed from those paths).

Two concurrent identical deliveries both read pre-success state → BOTH post T1 (postOrderPaid ledger.ts:154) + double notifications; LedgerTransaction has no refId unique (schema.prisma:725-733); NO $transaction anywhere in webhook path (grep: only checkout/create, claim/bind, addresses, register).

  1182→- Webhook pipeline is HMAC-verified and amount-checked but has a hardcoded demo secret, non-timing-safe compare, no replay window, no event-level unique constraint and no transaction — concurrent duplicate deliveries double-post ledger T1 and duplicate all notifications.
  1183→- /simulate is an unauthenticated "payment succeeded" switch (server self-signs); acceptable only as sandbox, and nothing enforces sandbox-only.

  1190→Task ID: AUDIT-A
  1191→Agent: Explore (ledger/wallet audit)
  1192→Task: Read-only audit of ledger & wallet integrity for financial-system gap analysis
  1193→
  1194→Work Log:
  1195→- Ledger core (src/lib/ledger.ts, full read): app-level double-entry balance check (debit==credit!=0, throws LEDGER_UNBALANCED) but NOT db-enforced; postTransaction does NOT use db.$transaction — per-line ensureAccount upserts + one nested-create, then separate refreshWalletCache; recipes are sequential awaited calls.
  1196→- ZERO idempotency at ledger level: LedgerTransaction has no @@unique on (txnType,refType,refId); webhook dedup is read-then-act on group/intent status → concurrent payment.succeeded can post T1 twice. No update/delete calls on ledger tables anywhere in src (immutability by convention only; LedgerLine has onDelete:Cascade).
  1197→- ACCOUNTS: BANK_MAIN, PAYOUT_CLEARING, REVENUE_COMMISSION, REVENUE_ADJUST, GATEWAY:{p}, SELLER:{w}:PAYABLE. No shipping-allocation/buyer-custody/chargeback accounts. postGatewaySettlement (T2) defined but never called → GATEWAY:* never clears to BANK_MAIN. postRefund supports partials but NEVER clamps to available balance — seller wallet can go negative. Chargeback: nothing; Dispute model is metadata-only, never posts ledger.
  1198→- refreshWalletCache/walletTruth: fully ledger-derived; available = credits with availableAt<=now − all debits (debits not gated by availableAt). Latent T1 imbalance if CommissionSetting.appliesToShipping=true (sellerNet+commission ≠ totalMinor when shipping>0 → LEDGER_UNBALANCED throw at payment time).
  1199→- Schema: Wallet(id,sellerId@unique,currency,pendingMinor,availableMinor,reservedMinor-dormant,lifetimeEarnedMinor,lifetimeCommissionMinor); no WalletEntry model; NO @@index/@@unique on LedgerTransaction/LedgerLine (walletTruth full-scans); all money Int minor; Floats are rates only (commissionPercent etc.). Payout.referenceNo @unique.
  1200→- Payout concurrency: approve/mark-paid/fail/reject all do JS status check → post ledger → UNCONDITIONAL payout.update where:{id}; no $transaction, no FOR UPDATE, no conditional update, no unique guard → double-approve double-spends T3 (negative seller balance); batch run same TOCTOU. Seller "one open request" (findFirst) raceable. Counter upsert (Prisma 6 native ON CONFLICT + increment) race-safe; referenceNo unique backstops.
  1201→- Payout statuses used: requested/processing/paid/failed/rejected only; transition guards are JS-only; no PSP-transfer vs bank-payout stage split (confirmed absent); Order.taxMinor never commissioned (always 0 today).
  1202→- Rounding: integer sen throughout; divHalfUp + allocateProportionally (remainder to largest weight); commission snapshots persisted per OrderItem at checkout, never recomputed; refund reversal uses amount×percent (ignores fixed-fee allocation) — minor asymmetry vs original commission.
  1203→- Fail-safe: /api/checkout/payments/[ref]/simulate is PUBLIC (no auth) and self-signs+processes payment.succeeded for any ref; webhook HMAC secret hardcoded "sm_webhook_secret_demo" in source; admin mark-paid records manual referenceNo with no provider evidence (RBAC+audit only). Only webhook process.ts and mark-paid write succeeded/paid (grep-verified).
  1204→
  1205→Stage Summary:
  1206→- Ledger is double-entry correct in app code but has NO db-level balance/idempotency constraints and NO $transaction wrapping — duplicate posting and torn writes are possible under concurrency.
  1207→- Payout approval is a TOCTOU chain (check→post→unconditional update); two concurrent approvals both pass and double-spend T3.
  1208→- Seller wallets can go negative via refunds (no clamp) and debits ignore availableAt; GATEWAY:* accounts never settle (T2 dead code).
  1209→- Schema money math is clean (Int minor everywhere, Floats only for rates) but ledger tables have zero indexes/unique constraints.
  1210→- Biggest production blockers: public self-signing simulate endpoint + hardcoded webhook secret = anyone can mint paid orders/T1 credits.
  1211→- Verified scripts/verify-ledger.ts runs runLedgerInvariants (balance + cache drift) as a post-migration gate; no chargeback/dispute-ledger linkage exists.

compatible lock order order→refund→wallet = no deadlock cycle) → conditional claim `updateMany({ where: { id, status: { not: "processed" } }, data: { status: "processed", processedAt } })` count 0 ⇒ 409 ALREADY_PROCESSED (FIN-2a pattern) → walletTruth(wallet.id, tx) BEFORE T5: availableMinor < amountMinor ⇒ 409 INSUFFICIENT_WALLET with the exact actionable message ("…Use Admin → Wallets → Adjust (T6) to add funds, then process again.") — throw rolls back the claim so NO posting and NO status flip (negative seller balances are never silently created; adjust→retry is the documented post-payout recovery path; check is deliberately conservative — requires the FULL refund amount even though T5 only debits the seller share, so the wallet always retains headroom vs concurrent reversals) → derive reversal from FRESH rows → postRefund({…, tx}) (dedupKey makes replays converge) → refund row commissionReversalMinor persisted in-tx (field the old flow stored; admin list/audit consumers) → transitionOrder(tx) → item refundedMinor bookkeeping + restock + inventoryLog on tx (fresh refundedMinor under the lock — never overwrites a sibling's allocation) → paymentIntent updateMany on tx.   1334→- ③ COMMISSION REVERSAL RULE (3a.2) — documented on the helper + in docs/finance-refund-policy.md + audit `reversalRule`: BREAKDOWN PATH (every item has a valid M09 breakdownJson snapshot): gross_i = lineSubtotal − discount_allocated + shipping_allocated (Σ gross = totalMinor per FIN-1 invariant); refund allocated across items proportional to gross_i via `allocateProportionally` (the SAME helper + remainder-to-largest rule checkout uses); reversal_i = divHalfUp(alloc_i × commissionMinor_i, gross_i) — the item's STORED commission (percent part + allocated fixed fee) at the same effective rate as the original order, restoring the fixed-fee component the old single-item-percent formula ignored; Σ clamped to [0, refundAmount] AND capped at max(0, Σ item.commissionMinor − Σ prior PROCESSED refunds' stored reversals) so cumulative reversals across partial refunds never exceed commission actually earned; full refund reproduces stored composition EXACTLY. FALLBACK (legacy/no-snapshot items): old rule byte-compatible — first item with remaining capacity, Math.round(refund × item.commissionPercent/100), no cap (historical behavior unchanged). Affects the NEW T5 only — stored history never rewritten.
  1335→- NEW GUARD (documented deviation): process on a refund whose status ≠ "approved" ⇒ 409 NOT_APPROVED. Previously a "requested" refund could be processed straight to money movement, bypassing the approval control; UI always approves first (orders.tsx:237-240), so no consumer breaks — this enforces approve→process segregation so the audit trail shows who authorized the money.
  1336→- approve/route.ts: kept STATUS-FLIP-ONLY (money moves at process); replaced the unconditional update with the FIN-2a conditional claim `updateMany({ where: { id, status: "requested" }, data: { status: "approved" } })`, count 0 ⇒ 409 CONFLICT (readable pre-check kept as fast-fail); response shape preserved by re-fetching the claimed row (`ok(updated)`); notify + audit byte-identical.

  1348→Stage Summary:
  1349→- The refund process step is now a single atomic, race-safe, idempotent money move: Order+Wallet row locks serialize per-order/per-wallet, the conditional claim rejects concurrent double-processing, the cumulative cap is re-validated against FRESH data (over-refund structurally impossible — proven with a crafted over row that the seller cap alone cannot produce), and wallet sufficiency is checked from ledger truth BEFORE T5 so a refund after payout can never silently drive a seller wallet negative — the documented recovery is the audited T6 adjust → retry path (never negative, never auto-clawback).

Rationale: refreshWalletCache computes truth from LedgerLine rows and only touches the Wallet row at UPDATE time, so the pre-fix balance re-verify could read pre-T5 ledger state while a refund tx (Order lock → Wallet lock → T5 insert) was in flight — approve passes its check on stale data and drives available negative. Lock-order analysis: refund = Order→Wallet (fixed order); approve = Wallet only (never touches Order/Refund rows); wallet adjust = Wallet row at its wallet.update (single row). Cross-flow conflicts are Wallet-only → no wait cycle possible; approve-vs-approve serializes on Wallet then resolves via the existing payout claim (loser 409).
